definition{
  name: appendChars,
  parameters: [a : (List String), b : (List String)],
  result: (List String),
  body: (match a
    | List.nil => b
    | List.cons h t => (cons h (appendChars t b))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: a
}

definition{
  name: charsEqual,
  parameters: [a : (List String), b : (List String)],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match a
    | List.nil => (match b
    | List.nil => true
    | List.cons h2 t2 => false)
    | List.cons h t => (match b
    | List.nil => false
    | List.cons h2 t2 => (if (prim equal h h2 : Bool) then (charsEqual t t2) else false))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: a
}

definition{
  name: isEmpty,
  parameters: [a : (List String)],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match a
    | List.nil => true
    | List.cons h t => false),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: memberString,
  parameters: [x : String, xs : (List String)],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match xs
    | List.nil => false
    | List.cons h t => (if (prim equal x h : Bool) then true else (memberString x t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: xs
}

definition{
  name: memberChars,
  parameters: [x : (List String), xs : (List (List String))],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match xs
    | List.nil => false
    | List.cons h t => (if (charsEqual x h) then true else (memberChars x t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: xs
}

definition{
  name: countOf,
  parameters: [xs : (List (List String))],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match xs
    | List.nil => 0
    | List.cons h t => ((countOf t) + 1)),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: xs
}

definition{
  name: digitOf,
  parameters: [n : Nat],
  result: String,
  body: (if (n =? 0) then "0" else (if (n =? 1) then "1" else (if (n =? 2) then "2" else (if (n =? 3) then "3" else (if (n =? 4) then "4" else (if (n =? 5) then "5" else (if (n =? 6) then "6" else (if (n =? 7) then "7" else (if (n =? 8) then "8" else "9"))))))))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: natChars,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, n : Nat],
  result: (List String),
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => (nil String)
    | Nat.succ f => (if (n <? 10) then (cons (digitOf n) (nil String)) else (appendChars (natChars f (prim quotient n 10 0 : Nat)) (cons (digitOf (prim remainder n 10 0 : Nat)) (nil String))))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: digits,
  parameters: [n : Nat],
  result: (List String),
  body: (natChars 32 n),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: accepted,
  parameters: [text : (List String)],
  result: Out,
  body: (record Out {ok := true, text := text}),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: refused,
  parameters: [text : (List String)],
  result: Out,
  body: (record Out {ok := false, text := text}),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: catOut,
  parameters: [a : Out, b : Out],
  result: Out,
  body: (if (project a ok) then (if (project b ok) then (accepted (appendChars (project a text) (project b text))) else b) else a),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: wrapParens,
  parameters: [o : Out, needed : Bool],
  result: Out,
  body: (if needed then (catOut (accepted (cons "(" (nil String))) (catOut o (accepted (cons ")" (nil String))))) else o),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: noNode,
  parameters: [],
  result: Node,
  body: (record Node {kind := "", atom := "", chars := (nil String), mode := "", left := 0, right := 0}),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: nodeAt,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node), k : Nat],
  result: Node,
  body: (match nodes
    | List.nil => (noNode)
    | List.cons h t => (match k
    | Nat.zero => h
    | Nat.succ j => (nodeAt t j))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: nodes
}

definition{
  name: countNodes,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node)],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match nodes
    | List.nil => 0
    | List.cons h t => ((countNodes t) + 1)),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: nodes
}

definition{
  name: fuelOf,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node)],
  result: Nat,
  body: ((((countNodes nodes) + (countNodes nodes)) + (countNodes nodes)) + 3),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: chainLength,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, nodes : (List Node), c : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => 0
    | Nat.succ f => (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes c) kind) "cons" : Bool) then ((chainLength f nodes (project (nodeAt nodes c) right)) + 1) else 0)),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: chainDrop,
  parameters: [n : Nat, nodes : (List Node), c : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match n
    | Nat.zero => c
    | Nat.succ m => (chainDrop m nodes (project (nodeAt nodes c) right))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: n
}

definition{
  name: chainNth,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node), c : Nat, n : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (project (nodeAt nodes (chainDrop n nodes c)) left),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: headOf,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node), k : Nat],
  result: Node,
  body: (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes k) kind) "app" : Bool) then (nodeAt nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) left)) else (nodeAt nodes k)),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: isAppOf,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node), k : Nat, name : String],
  result: Bool,
  body: (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes k) kind) "app" : Bool) then (if (prim equal (project (headOf nodes k) kind) "const" : Bool) then (prim equal (project (headOf nodes k) atom) name : Bool) else false) else false),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: sortQid,
  parameters: [n : Node],
  result: String,
  body: (if (prim equal (project n kind) "const" : Bool) then (if (prim equal (project n atom) "Nat" : Bool) then "lexlean.std.nat::nat" else (if (prim equal (project n atom) "Int" : Bool) then "lexlean.std.int::int" else (if (prim equal (project n atom) "Bool" : Bool) then "lexlean.std.bool::bool" else ""))) else ""),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: isVarType,
  parameters: [n : Node],
  result: Bool,
  body: (if (prim equal (sortQid n) "" : Bool) then false else true),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: isBinder,
  parameters: [n : Node],
  result: Bool,
  body: (if (prim equal (project n kind) "pi" : Bool) then true else (prim equal (project n kind) "lam" : Bool)),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: isDataResult,
  parameters: [n : Node],
  result: Bool,
  body: (if (if (prim equal (project n kind) "const" : Bool) then (if (prim equal (sortQid n) "" : Bool) then false else true) else false) then true else (if (prim equal (project n kind) "sort" : Bool) then (if (if (prim equal (project n atom) "prop" : Bool) then true else (prim equal (project n atom) "0" : Bool)) then false else true) else false)),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: piResult,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, nodes : (List Node), k : Nat],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => k
    | Nat.succ f => (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes k) kind) "pi" : Bool) then (piResult f nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right)) else k)),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: isFunctionType,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node), k : Nat],
  result: Bool,
  body: (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes k) kind) "pi" : Bool) then (isDataResult (nodeAt nodes (piResult (fuelOf nodes) nodes k))) else false),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: chainHasOfNat,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, nodes : (List Node), c : Nat],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => false
    | Nat.succ f => (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes c) kind) "cons" : Bool) then (if (isAppOf nodes (project (nodeAt nodes c) left) "OfNat.ofNat") then true else (chainHasOfNat f nodes (project (nodeAt nodes c) right))) else false)),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: numeralUnderEq,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, nodes : (List Node), k : Nat],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => false
    | Nat.succ f => (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes k) kind) "app" : Bool) then (if (if (isAppOf nodes k "Eq") then (chainHasOfNat f nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right)) else false) then true else (if (numeralUnderEq f nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) left)) then true else (numeralUnderEq f nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right)))) else (if (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes k) kind) "cons" : Bool) then true else (isBinder (nodeAt nodes k))) then (if (numeralUnderEq f nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) left)) then true else (numeralUnderEq f nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right))) else false))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: chainHasVar,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, nodes : (List Node), k : Nat],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => false
    | Nat.succ f => (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes k) kind) "pi" : Bool) then (if (isVarType (nodeAt nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) left))) then true else (chainHasVar f nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right))) else false)),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: hasDotAt,
  parameters: [cs : (List String)],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match cs
    | List.nil => false
    | List.cons a t1 => (match t1
    | List.nil => false
    | List.cons b t2 => (match t2
    | List.nil => false
    | List.cons c t3 => (if (if (prim equal a "." : Bool) then (if (prim equal b "_" : Bool) then (prim equal c "@" : Bool) else false) else false) then true else (hasDotAt t1))))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: cs
}

definition{
  name: sanitize,
  parameters: [cs : (List String)],
  result: (List String),
  body: (match cs
    | List.nil => (nil String)
    | List.cons c t => (if (prim equal c "'" : Bool) then (appendChars (cons "p" (cons "r" (cons "i" (cons "m" (cons "e" (nil String)))))) (sanitize t)) else (if (memberString c (alphanumerics)) then (cons c (sanitize t)) else (sanitize t)))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: cs
}

definition{
  name: isAnonymous,
  parameters: [n : Node],
  result: Bool,
  body: (if (hasDotAt (project n chars)) then true else (if (prim equal (project n atom) "[anonymous]" : Bool) then true else (prim equal (project n atom) "" : Bool))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: baseName,
  parameters: [n : Node],
  result: (List String),
  body: (if (isAnonymous n) then (nil String) else (sanitize (project n chars))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: usesCounter,
  parameters: [n : Node],
  result: Bool,
  body: (if (isBinder n) then (isEmpty (baseName n)) else false),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: dedupe,
  parameters: [fuel : Nat, name : (List String), bound : (List (List String))],
  result: (List String),
  body: (match fuel
    | Nat.zero => name
    | Nat.succ f => (if (memberChars name bound) then (dedupe f (appendChars name (cons "0" (nil String))) bound) else name)),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: fuel
}

definition{
  name: assignBase,
  parameters: [n : Node, counter : Nat],
  result: (List String),
  body: (if (isEmpty (baseName n)) then (cons "h" (digits (counter + 1))) else (baseName n)),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: assignReserved,
  parameters: [name : (List String), hn : Bool],
  result: (List String),
  body: (if (if hn then (charsEqual name (cons "a" (nil String))) else false) then (appendChars name (cons "0" (nil String))) else name),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: assign,
  parameters: [n : Node, bound : (List (List String)), counter : Nat, hn : Bool],
  result: (List String),
  body: (dedupe ((countOf bound) + 1) (assignReserved (assignBase n counter) hn) bound),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: anonUpTo,
  parameters: [k : Nat, nodes : (List Node)],
  result: Nat,
  body: (match k
    | Nat.zero => 0
    | Nat.succ j => ((anonUpTo j nodes) + (if (usesCounter (nodeAt nodes j)) then 1 else 0))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: k
}

definition{
  name: extendNames,
  parameters: [n : Node, prev : (List (List String)), counter : Nat, hn : Bool],
  result: (List (List String)),
  body: (if (isBinder n) then (prim append prev (cons (assign n prev counter hn) (nil (List String))) : (List (List String))) else prev),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: namesUpTo,
  parameters: [k : Nat, nodes : (List Node), hn : Bool],
  result: (List (List String)),
  body: (match k
    | Nat.zero => (nil (List String))
    | Nat.succ j => (extendNames (nodeAt nodes j) (namesUpTo j nodes hn) (anonUpTo j nodes) hn)),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: k
}

definition{
  name: nameAt,
  parameters: [k : Nat, nodes : (List Node), hn : Bool],
  result: (List String),
  body: (assign (nodeAt nodes k) (namesUpTo k nodes hn) (anonUpTo k nodes) hn),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: localName,
  parameters: [n : Node, env : (List Binder)],
  result: (List String),
  body: (match env
    | List.nil => (project n chars)
    | List.cons b t => (if (prim equal (project b raw) (project n atom) : Bool) then (project b name) else (localName n t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: env
}

definition{
  name: localSort,
  parameters: [raw : String, env : (List Binder)],
  result: String,
  body: (match env
    | List.nil => ""
    | List.cons b t => (if (prim equal (project b raw) raw : Bool) then (project b sort) else (localSort raw t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: env
}

definition{
  name: bindAt,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node), k : Nat, env : (List Binder), hn : Bool],
  result: (List Binder),
  body: (cons (record Binder {raw := (project (nodeAt nodes k) atom), name := (nameAt k nodes hn), sort := (sortQid (nodeAt nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) left)))}) env),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: noLexeme,
  parameters: [],
  result: Lexeme,
  body: (record Lexeme {qid := "", qidChars := (nil String), lean := "", surface := "", surfaceChars := (nil String), hasSurface := false, frame := "", arity := 0, precedence := 0, associativity := "", sort := "", visible := false, builtin := false}),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: lookupVisible,
  parameters: [lean : String, rows : (List Lexeme)],
  result: Found,
  body: (match rows
    | List.nil => (record Found {ok := false, lexeme := (noLexeme)})
    | List.cons h t => (if (if (prim equal (project h lean) lean : Bool) then (if (project h visible) then true else (project h builtin)) else false) then (record Found {ok := true, lexeme := h}) else (lookupVisible lean t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: rows
}

definition{
  name: lookupAny,
  parameters: [lean : String, rows : (List Lexeme)],
  result: Found,
  body: (match rows
    | List.nil => (record Found {ok := false, lexeme := (noLexeme)})
    | List.cons h t => (if (prim equal (project h lean) lean : Bool) then (record Found {ok := true, lexeme := h}) else (lookupAny lean t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: rows
}

definition{
  name: pickFound,
  parameters: [a : Found, b : Found],
  result: Found,
  body: (if (project a ok) then a else b),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: lookupLean,
  parameters: [lean : String],
  result: Found,
  body: (pickFound (lookupVisible lean (lexicon)) (lookupAny lean (lexicon))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: lookupQid,
  parameters: [qid : String, rows : (List Lexeme)],
  result: Lexeme,
  body: (match rows
    | List.nil => (noLexeme)
    | List.cons h t => (if (prim equal (project h qid) qid : Bool) then h else (lookupQid qid t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: rows
}

definition{
  name: carrierHas,
  parameters: [op : String, rows : (List Carrier)],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match rows
    | List.nil => false
    | List.cons h t => (if (prim equal (project h operation) op : Bool) then true else (carrierHas op t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: rows
}

definition{
  name: carrierFind,
  parameters: [op : String, c : String, rows : (List Carrier)],
  result: Carried,
  body: (match rows
    | List.nil => (record Carried {ok := false, lean := "", typeArguments := 0})
    | List.cons h t => (if (if (prim equal (project h operation) op : Bool) then (prim equal (project h carrier) c : Bool) else false) then (record Carried {ok := true, lean := (project h lean), typeArguments := (project h typeArguments)}) else (carrierFind op c t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: rows
}

definition{
  name: hintFor,
  parameters: [lean : String, rows : (List Hint)],
  result: (List String),
  body: (match rows
    | List.nil => (nil String)
    | List.cons h t => (if (prim equal (project h lean) lean : Bool) then (project h hint) else (hintFor lean t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: rows
}

definition{
  name: hintPart,
  parameters: [shown : String, lean : String],
  result: (List String),
  body: (if (isEmpty (hintFor shown (hints))) then (if (isEmpty (hintFor lean (hints))) then (nil String) else (appendChars (cons ";" (cons " " (nil String))) (hintFor lean (hints)))) else (appendChars (cons ";" (cons " " (nil String))) (hintFor shown (hints)))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: rewriteOf,
  parameters: [lean : String, rows : (List Rewrite)],
  result: String,
  body: (match rows
    | List.nil => ""
    | List.cons h t => (if (prim equal (project h lean) lean : Bool) then (project h target) else (rewriteOf lean t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: rows
}

definition{
  name: rewriteChars,
  parameters: [lean : String, rows : (List Rewrite)],
  result: (List String),
  body: (match rows
    | List.nil => (nil String)
    | List.cons h t => (if (prim equal (project h lean) lean : Bool) then (project h targetChars) else (rewriteChars lean t))),
  axioms: [],
  recursive_argument: rows
}

definition{
  name: sortWord,
  parameters: [qid : String],
  result: (List String),
  body: (if (prim equal qid "lexlean.std.nat::nat" : Bool) then (cons "n" (cons "a" (cons "t" (cons "u" (cons "r" (cons "a" (cons "l" (cons " " (cons "n" (cons "u" (cons "m" (cons "b" (cons "e" (cons "r" (nil String))))))))))))))) else (if (prim equal qid "lexlean.std.int::int" : Bool) then (cons "i" (cons "n" (cons "t" (cons "e" (cons "g" (cons "e" (cons "r" (nil String)))))))) else (if (prim equal qid "lexlean.std.bool::bool" : Bool) then (cons "t" (cons "r" (cons "u" (cons "t" (cons "h" (cons " " (cons "v" (cons "a" (cons "l" (cons "u" (cons "e" (nil String)))))))))))) else (nil String)))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: sortArticle,
  parameters: [qid : String],
  result: (List String),
  body: (if (prim equal qid "lexlean.std.nat::nat" : Bool) then (cons "a" (cons " " (nil String))) else (if (prim equal qid "lexlean.std.int::int" : Bool) then (cons "a" (cons "n" (cons " " (nil String)))) else (if (prim equal qid "lexlean.std.bool::bool" : Bool) then (cons "a" (cons " " (nil String))) else (nil String)))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: lexemeHead,
  parameters: [lean : String, n : Nat, shown : String, shownChars : (List String), skip : Nat, found : Found],
  result: Head,
  body: (if (if (project found ok) then false else true) then (record Head {kind := "refuse", lexeme := (noLexeme), skip := 0, text := (appendChars (textNoLexeme) (appendChars shownChars (appendChars (cons "`" (nil String)) (hintPart shown lean))))}) else (if (if (if (project (project found lexeme) visible) then true else (project (project found lexeme) builtin)) then false else true) then (record Head {kind := "refuse", lexeme := (noLexeme), skip := 0, text := (appendChars (cons "`" (nil String)) (appendChars (project (project found lexeme) qidChars) (textNotVisible)))}) else (if (if ((project (project found lexeme) arity) =? n) then false else true) then (record Head {kind := "refuse", lexeme := (noLexeme), skip := 0, text := (appendChars (cons "`" (nil String)) (appendChars (project (project found lexeme) qidChars) (appendChars (textTakes) (appendChars (digits (project (project found lexeme) arity)) (appendChars (textArgumentsGot) (digits n))))))}) else (record Head {kind := "lexeme", lexeme := (project found lexeme), skip := skip, text := (nil String)})))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: resolveLexeme,
  parameters: [lean : String, n : Nat, shown : String, shownChars : (List String), skip : Nat],
  result: Head,
  body: (lexemeHead lean n shown shownChars skip (lookupLean lean)),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: resolveCarrier,
  parameters: [name : String, nameChars : (List String), n : Nat, c : Node, found : Carried],
  result: Head,
  body: (if (project found ok) then (resolveLexeme (project found lean) (prim subtract n ((project found typeArguments) + 1) : Nat) name nameChars ((project found typeArguments) + 1)) else (record Head {kind := "refuse", lexeme := (noLexeme), skip := 0, text := (appendChars nameChars (appendChars (cons " " (cons "o" (cons "v" (cons "e" (cons "r" (cons " " (nil String))))))) (if (prim equal (project c kind) "const" : Bool) then (project c chars) else (cons "N" (cons "o" (cons "n" (cons "e" (nil String))))))))})),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: resolvePlain,
  parameters: [name : String, nameChars : (List String), n : Nat],
  result: Head,
  body: (if (if (if (prim equal (rewriteOf name (defeqs)) "" : Bool) then false else true) then (project (lookupAny (rewriteOf name (defeqs)) (lexicon)) ok) else false) then (resolveLexeme (rewriteOf name (defeqs)) n name nameChars 0) else (resolveLexeme name n name nameChars 0)),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: resolveNamed,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node), k : Nat, name : String, nameChars : (List String), n : Nat],
  result: Head,
  body: (if (prim equal name "OfNat.ofNat" : Bool) then (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes (chainNth nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right) 1)) kind) "nat" : Bool) then (record Head {kind := "nat", lexeme := (noLexeme), skip := 0, text := (project (nodeAt nodes (chainNth nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right) 1)) chars)}) else (record Head {kind := "refuse", lexeme := (noLexeme), skip := 0, text := (textNonLiteralOfNat)})) else (if (if (if (prim equal (sortQid (headOf nodes k)) "" : Bool) then false else true) then (n =? 0) else false) then (record Head {kind := "lexeme", lexeme := (lookupQid (sortQid (headOf nodes k)) (lexicon)), skip := 0, text := (nil String)}) else (if (if (prim equal name "Nat.zero" : Bool) then (n =? 0) else false) then (record Head {kind := "nat", lexeme := (noLexeme), skip := 0, text := (cons "0" (nil String))}) else (if (prim equal name "Eq" : Bool) then (record Head {kind := "lexeme", lexeme := (lookupQid "lexlean.core::eq" (lexicon)), skip := 1, text := (nil String)}) else (if (prim equal name "And" : Bool) then (record Head {kind := "lexeme", lexeme := (lookupQid "lexlean.core::land" (lexicon)), skip := 0, text := (nil String)}) else (if (prim equal name "Or" : Bool) then (record Head {kind := "lexeme", lexeme := (lookupQid "lexlean.core::lor" (lexicon)), skip := 0, text := (nil String)}) else (if (prim equal name "Iff" : Bool) then (record Head {kind := "lexeme", lexeme := (lookupQid "lexlean.core::iff" (lexicon)), skip := 0, text := (nil String)}) else (if (prim equal name "Not" : Bool) then (record Head {kind := "lexeme", lexeme := (lookupQid "lexlean.core::lnot" (lexicon)), skip := 0, text := (nil String)}) else (if (prim equal name "Ne" : Bool) then (record Head {kind := "ne", lexeme := (lookupQid "lexlean.core::lnot" (lexicon)), skip := 1, text := (nil String)}) else (if (prim equal name "Exists" : Bool) then (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes (chainNth nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right) 1)) kind) "lam" : Bool) then (record Head {kind := "exists", lexeme := (lookupQid "lexlean.core::exists-op" (lexicon)), skip := 1, text := (nil String)}) else (record Head {kind := "refuse", lexeme := (noLexeme), skip := 0, text := (textExistsBinder)})) else (if (carrierHas name (carriers)) then (resolveCarrier name nameChars n (nodeAt nodes (chainNth nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right) 0)) (carrierFind name (if (prim equal (project (nodeAt nodes (chainNth nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right) 0)) kind) "const" : Bool) then (project (nodeAt nodes (chainNth nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right) 0)) atom) else "None") (carriers))) else (if (if (prim equal (rewriteOf name (aliases)) "" : Bool) then false else true) then (resolvePlain (rewriteOf name (aliases)) (rewriteChars name (aliases)) n) else (resolvePlain name nameChars n))))))))))))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: resolveHead,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node), k : Nat],
  result: Head,
  body: (if (prim equal (project (headOf nodes k) kind) "const" : Bool) then (resolveNamed nodes k (project (headOf nodes k) atom) (project (headOf nodes k) chars) (chainLength (fuelOf nodes) nodes (project (nodeAt nodes k) right))) else (record Head {kind := "refuse", lexeme := (noLexeme), skip := 0, text := (textNonConstantHead)})),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: sortOf,
  parameters: [nodes : (List Node), env : (List Binder), k : Nat],
